## 11. Risks

| Risk | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| The ImGui look falls short of what the owner wants from the SeverActions spirit (typography) | S4 draws a Grace page in game early and the owner reviews screenshots beside the browser mock; a kerning routine for display text if asked (§3.16); fallbacks in G1 (RmlUi drawn as ImGui geometry, or an Ultralight spike) |
| The two views drift | Pages as data (§2.5), one token file, generated protocol types, parity goldens (§9.3) |
| The atlas grows too large at 4K and 150% | The face budget and rebuild cap (D29); S4 measures |
| The overlay libraries' own input and texture paths: Dear ImGui's Win32 backend polls XInput itself, and its DX12 backend updates textures during play when a font atlas grows (since 1.92) | The Win32 backend built without its gamepad code (§5.4); the atlas baked at warm-up and never grown (§3.16); the host's `WM_CHAR` surrogate pairing; no texture updates after load, the map's on a piece gained alone, counted (D28, D76, G26) |
| An attack on the local server, now always on | The checks and suite of §7.2; keys bound to their address (D61, EldenNet's); only listed files served; other devices kept out unless paired (G9) |
| A spoiler through the UI | Projections only (invariant 1; EldenNet's SP1), EldenNet's goldens per fixture state (its PAGES §5), absence silent (invariant 2) |
| An input leak: Esc opens the game's menu, B dodges | The mask-until-release rule, the close matrix, tests on pad and keyboard |
| Proton and the Deck: IME, Steam keyboard, opening a browser under gamescope | On EldenNet's matrix; on a Deck in game mode the in-game menu is the path, and the dashboard is for desktop mode |
| Scope creep (editors, explorers, sound) | Seams only in v1 (§1.3) |
| The in-process bridge's cost: nlohmann/json parses a 250 KiB state frame in about 3.3 ms and writes it in 0.7 ms on our build machine (D159), against §8's 0.5 ms each for the largest payload (`kinds`, EldenNet's v0.4) | grace-protocol already copies nothing of a frame's body; S4 measures on the player's hardware; if it stays over, a faster parser behind grace-protocol's readers (yyjson, MIT, is the first to try), or `publish` checking a body's syntax and passing its bytes through, parsed only by the view that draws it (a D-row then amends D55) |
| A framework-sized rail (twelve top-level entries, thirteen with Plugins from 1.1, and 30 pages) is slow to cross on a pad | The accordion keeps one category open; LT and RT walk every page in order; search reaches any setting and Quick switches the main ones; the rail collapses on small screens (§4.2.2, §5.4) |
| Players expect SkyrimNet's pages that EldenNet does not have | Later EldenNet features are statement pages tagged "WIP" (G7); Docs explains what EldenNet does instead; not-planned pages are simply absent (EldenNet's PAGES §4.11) |
| A second home creeps in through Settings | Settings holds only homeless settings and links out (D40); the page lint fails a key with two homes |
| Mixed or stale GraceUI copies after a manual update | `graceui.toml` with sizes and hashes, `connect`'s answers, majors that must match, EldenNet's launcher installing or explaining, notices in words; a second copy idles (§2.9, D83, D88) |
| The ABI drifts between releases | Append-only tables with sizes, a CI check against the last release's header, the connect tests both ways round (§9.1); ABI 1 a preview until GraceUI 1.1 (G18 (a)) |
| A public ABI's support burden outlives EldenNet's needs | A preview until GraceUI 1.1 (G18 (a)); append-only tables; a stated window per major (D74); the release header check |
| EldenNet v0.1 now waits on GraceUI's host | The host is mostly the glue EldenNet's own overlay would have had, built in GraceUI 0.1 from the same spike work (S0-E); EldenNet's side is its banner as a `notice` call and F11's frame glue on a headless context |
| A third-party mod's fault (a panel, an `on_present` callback) stops EldenNet's surfaces or the game, and is reported against GraceUI or EldenNet | Budgets and suspension; checked draw lists; the bug-report checklist names every consumer; documented: native mods share one process (§7.1) |
| Another hooking overlay beside GraceUI goes silently dead, or kills GraceUI's | Documented in the guide and the matrix; the install-time warning (D86); cannot be prevented by GraceUI |
| The server becomes a port for every mod's commands | Loopback only, mods opt in, no mod web code, per-space routing, sensitive commands, the server suite with several spaces (D81) |
| Modders used to HTML hosts find page data too narrow | Panels for anything else; the vocabulary grows by minors; HTML in game stays G1's gated spike |
| Scope creep against EldenNet's "minimal and stable" | The framework's additions are designed now and opened with GraceUI 1.1, after EldenNet v1.0 (G18 (a)); EldenNet uses only what it needs |
| Reception of an AI-built framework | The disclosure, the reviewed design, the tests in the open; no presence where AI is refused (D87) |
| Texture and atlas budgets shared across mods | Per-mod budgets, a registration window, the limits logged (D76, D80) |
| Name and download impersonation | Hashes on release pages; one canonical page (D87) |
| The map: no run-time route to the player's own map | The drawn base is the map (designed in); the research found the tiles and their masks, leaving EldenNet's own read of the game's archives (§4.13) |
| The map key cannot be caught cleanly on every input path | The setting stays off where it cannot (G25 defaults to off) |
| The map's video memory | The in-game texture capped (§4.13) and measured; each re-upload counted (G26) |
| A spoiler through the map | Every base only what the player's own game map shows (D90); the layers masked in EldenNet; goldens that render the map at fixture states |
| A port clash with another local tool | Fail closed with a toast naming the port and where to change it; the default 8642 avoids the ports of the local model servers EldenNet probes (D58) |

---

## 12. Decisions for the owner

**Status: the owner decided G1–G10 and G12 on 2026-11-07 and G13 on 2026-11-08, and made the request G11 records; G14
widened GraceUI's scope, and its consequences were put as G15–G20 with proposal 0001: G15–G17 decided the same day
(superseding G10), and G18–G20 on 2026-11-16, as recommended; G21–G26 were put with proposal 0002 (the map) on
2026-11-14 and decided on 2026-11-15 (G23 not as recommended); G27 decided on 2026-11-14; G28 put and decided on
2026-11-15, as recommended, for EldenNet's plugins (its O67); G29 put on 2026-11-15 with EldenNet's proposal 0002 and
decided on 2026-11-16, as recommended; G30 put on 2026-11-16 with the re-base on C++ and decided the same day, as
recommended, after an independent review (SRV), the day the owner also made G15's overlay Dear ImGui with MinHook (its
later change). G31 and G32 were put on 2026-11-20 at the look review of the in-game menu and decided the same day, as
recommended; G33 was put the same day with proposal 0006 and decided the same day, as recommended; G34 was put on
2026-11-21 with proposal 0007 and decided the same day, as recommended; G35, proposal 0005 (put on 2026-11-20 and
entered here with its answer), was decided on 2026-11-21, as proposed; G36 was put on 2026-11-21 with proposal 0008 and
decided the same day, as recommended; G37 was put on 2026-11-22 with proposal 0009 and decided the same day, as
recommended; G38 was put on 2026-11-22 with proposal 0010 and decided the same day, as recommended; G39 was put on
2026-11-23 with proposal 0011 and decided the same day against it: no change in GraceUI; G40 and G41 were raised by
the owner and decided on 2026-11-23, as recommended.**
Every decision is recorded in full in [DECISIONS.md](DECISIONS.md), the one record: the question as it was put, the
options, the recommendation as written, what was decided, the owner's words and what each amends. This section is its
index; each G-number the documents cite is a row here and an entry there. G11 and G12 were not questions of §12: they
were first recorded in §14 and CLAUDE.md, and are indexed here like the rest. G1, G2, G4, G5, G6, G8, G9, G10, G15–G17,
G19–G27, G29 and G30 also touch EldenNet's design; [eldennet-changes.md](eldennet-changes.md) lists what each answer
changes there. G15–G17 and G21–G27 were folded into this design on 2026-11-15 (EldenNet's roadmap S7.8.c), G30 on
2026-11-16, and G18–G20 and G29 the same day (S7.8.d and S7.12.e). A question that comes up later is added to
DECISIONS.md as a new entry marked **Open**, with a recommendation, and as a row here; the owner's answer is recorded
in the entry with its date, and a later decision amends an earlier one by a note, never by rewriting it.

| # | Question | Decision, in brief | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| [G1](DECISIONS.md#g1) | Which renderer draws the menu in game? | (a) Dear ImGui with the Grace theme in EldenNet's one overlay; the full web UI in the browser (the overlay GraceUI's since G15; no HTML in game for any mod, G16) | Decided 2026-11-07 |
| [G2](DECISIONS.md#g2) | A library inside EldenNet, or a standalone mod? | A standalone mod; each EldenNet release carries the current one | Decided 2026-11-07 |
| [G3](DECISIONS.md#g3) | Which framework for the browser app? | (b) Preact with TypeScript and signals, built with Vite | Decided 2026-11-07 |
| [G4](DECISIONS.md#g4) | Is the browser dashboard on by default? | (b) always on, on this PC; off game too while EldenNet's launcher runs, since G29 | Decided 2026-11-07; widened by G29 on 2026-11-16 |
| [G5](DECISIONS.md#g5) | NPC types: are types listed before the player has seen one? | (a) every type listed from the start, first-meeting profiles only | Decided 2026-11-07; widened the same day |
| [G6](DECISIONS.md#g6) | Should the Steam Deck default to a larger text scale? | (b) `ui.scale = 1.35` on a Steam Deck | Decided 2026-11-07 |
| [G7](DECISIONS.md#g7) | Do later-version features appear in the sidebar? | (b) dimmed entries tagged "WIP" | Decided 2026-11-07 |
| [G8](DECISIONS.md#g8) | Are unmet published personas listed? | Every named NPC's general profile from the start; a new Playthrough page | Decided 2026-11-07; widened the same day |
| [G9](DECISIONS.md#g9) | May other devices on the home network open the dashboard? | (b) a switch, off by default, and pairing with a code shown in game | Decided 2026-11-07 |
| [G10](DECISIONS.md#g10) | Which module owns the one overlay? | (b) EldenNet keeps it; `graceui.dll` hooks nothing | Decided 2026-11-07; superseded by G15 on 2026-11-08 |
| [G11](DECISIONS.md#g11) | The owner's request that started GraceUI | A separate repository; SkyrimNet's layout; a look in SeverActions' spirit; an AI framework's categories | The owner's request, 2026-11-07 (§14) |
| [G12](DECISIONS.md#g12) | SkyrimNet | Its code, dashboard and styling may be used and adapted, credited as Min wishes; since G19, for EldenNet's own pages only, never in GraceUI | Decided 2026-11-07 (CLAUDE.md); narrowed by G19 on 2026-11-16 |
| [G13](DECISIONS.md#g13) | How is an interactive showcase of the menu built? | (d) Wait for Min's blueprint; the static showcase stays; it is EldenNet's, not GraceUI's | Decided 2026-11-08 |
| [G14](DECISIONS.md#g14) | GraceUI's scope: does the browser view stay in GraceUI? | Widened: a UI framework other modders can use, as Magelight UI and PrismaUI are for Skyrim; EldenNet requires and ships it. Consequences to be put as G15 onwards | Decided 2026-11-08 (scope) |
| [G15](DECISIONS.md#g15) | Who owns the one overlay? | (a) GraceUI owns it, so any mod can use it without EldenNet; supersedes G10. Since 2026-11-16 the overlay is Dear ImGui with MinHook, in hudhook's place (EldenNet O25) | Decided 2026-11-08; its libraries changed by the owner on 2026-11-16 |
| [G16](DECISIONS.md#g16) | What do other mods write, and what draws it in game? | (a) page data plus checked panels; no HTML in game | Decided 2026-11-08 |
| [G17](DECISIONS.md#g17) | Is the browser view a GraceUI feature for every mod? | (a) yes, for mods that opt in | Decided 2026-11-08 |
| [G18](DECISIONS.md#g18) | When does the framework open to other modders? | (a) after EldenNet v1.0, as GraceUI 1.1 | Decided 2026-11-16, as recommended |
| [G19](DECISIONS.md#g19) | May SkyrimNet-derived material reach other mods through GraceUI? | (a) not until Min confirms: GraceUI keeps its own code and theme | Decided 2026-11-16, as recommended |
| [G20](DECISIONS.md#g20) | Where do EldenNet's pages live? | (a) in EldenNet's repository, registered with GraceUI | Decided 2026-11-16, as recommended |
| [G21](DECISIONS.md#g21) | What is the map drawn on? | (a) the player's own game map, its tiles composed as the game composes them for the pieces held, read at run time, never shipped; the drawn base as fallback | Decided 2026-11-15 |
| [G22](DECISIONS.md#g22) | May anything on the map loop or pulse? | (a) no; still glow and one-shot flares (invariant 14 unchanged) | Decided 2026-11-15 |
| [G23](DECISIONS.md#g23) | Who writes the lore notes? | (c) the model adds notes on its own, citing memories; the player edits or removes them; a setting, on by default | Decided 2026-11-15 |
| [G24](DECISIONS.md#g24) | When does the map ship? | (a) after v1.0, in 1.1; the spike early | Decided 2026-11-15 |

| [G26](DECISIONS.md#g26) | In game, how is the game's map kept to what the player has reached? | (a) as the game shows it: composed at start and again when a map piece is gained; a measured exception to invariant 11 | Decided 2026-11-15 |
| [G27](DECISIONS.md#g27) | How does the map show terrain? | (a) the exact terrain built locally: a 3D relief in the browser, a relief image in game; our own sprites and the tile-grid schematic as the fallback | Decided 2026-11-14 |
| [G28](DECISIONS.md#g28) | May a space take a file from the browser view? | (a) a narrow upload route on the loopback listener, for a space's sensitive file commands alone, refused from paired devices | Decided 2026-11-15 |
| [G29](DECISIONS.md#g29) | GraceUI's server for hosts outside the game? | (a) one server library GraceUI publishes, with every rule of §7.2 and invariant 4 | Decided 2026-11-16, as recommended |

| [G31](DECISIONS.md#g31) | What pointer does the menu draw in game? | (a) our own pointer in the Grace look, still: a notched arrowhead with rounded corners, a gold edge on a dark fill (D178) | Decided 2026-11-20, as recommended |

| [G35](DECISIONS.md#g35) | Pad chords for hotkeys (proposal 0005)? | As proposed: an anchor then a key (EldenNet's D-pad Up, then View), 150 ms and 0.4 s EST, standing aside when input would be refused, ABI 1.3 | Decided 2026-11-21, as proposed; built 2026-11-22 (D204, GraceUI 0.2.14) |
| [G37](DECISIONS.md#g37) | Where and how a hotkey is rebound (proposal 0009)? | Recommended: on GraceUI's Hotkeys page for every mod, kept under the mod's and the action's ids; a taken key asked, then moved; chords from two pickers; a mod may read its current key (ABI 1.4) | Decided 2026-11-22, as recommended; built as D208 to D210 (GraceUI 0.2.16) |

| [G38](DECISIONS.md#g38) | What the profile editor needs from GraceUI (proposal 0010)? | Recommended: topic families, master–detail in both views, an editor block, slow commands and the text field in game, all now in 0.2.x, text field first; unsaved edits kept in memory when the menu closes; prose as one paragraph | Decided 2026-11-22: all three, as recommended; to be built in 0.2.x, the text field first |
| [G39](DECISIONS.md#g39) | May a mod take one Escape press after its box has closed (proposal 0011)? | Recommended: yes, armed by the mod while its reply paces, one press, at most 60 s, Escape only, answered at once and disarmed by anything that matters more; the keyboard only, not the pad's B | Decided 2026-11-23: (c), no: Esc and the pad act on a chat box only while it is open, and are the game's again once it closes; no change in GraceUI |

## 13. Decision log

Settled by this design (2026-11-07) within the owner's request, EldenNet's decided rules and the research. Each can be
reopened by the owner. D69 to D98 were settled in proposal 0001 (the framework, 2026-11-08) and proposal 0002 (the map,
2026-11-14) and adopted with the owner's answers G15–G17 and G21–G27 on 2026-11-15 (EldenNet's roadmap S7.8.c); the
proposals keep their full reasoning; D99 to D101 were settled by the fold itself, D102 and D103 follow EldenNet's O65
to O67, and D104 and D105 its O70 (2026-11-15). D106 to D110 were settled by the re-base on C++ (2026-11-16,
EldenNet's O60 and roadmap S8.1.e), which changed the entries above only where they named Rust or hudhook, and D111
to D114 when G30 was folded in the same day, from the independent review (SRV). D82 (EldenNet's pages in EldenNet's
repository) came into force with G20 (a) on 2026-11-16, when §4's EldenNet content and its rules moved to EldenNet's
PAGES, and D61 with them (EldenNet's roadmap S7.8.d). D116 to D123 were settled by the first code, the render half of
GraceUI's 0.1 host (2026-11-16, EldenNet's roadmap S10.4.a; `BUILD.md`), and D124 to D130 by its input half the same
day; D131 to D136 by S10.4.b the same day: ABI 1 as a preview, the anti-cheat check and its notice-only mode, notices,
the second copy and `graceui-sys`; D137 to D144 by S10.4.c the same day: panels and their checked draw lists,
textures at load, faces and glyph tables, the notices' Grace look, input and world-state providers through ABI 1, and
the second example mod; and D145 to D153 by the independent review of the host the same day (its three Medium findings
M1 to M3 and fourteen Low ones, L1 to L14, each fixed; what a fix leaves for later is named in its row), which amend
the entries above by dated notes; and D154, the harness's frame-cost checks under a loaded machine, the same day.

| # | Decision | Choice | Alternatives | Why | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | Repository | GraceUI is a separate repository; EldenNet depends on it, never the reverse | Inside EldenNet | The owner's choice; the UI has its own toolchain (TypeScript) and release rhythm | Owner, 2026-11-07 |
| D2 | Layout | SkyrimNet's dashboard idea: a home with status and live activity, searchable settings, diagnostics, an event monitor. Not in v1: a game-data explorer, content editors, agents, a content hub. | D54 | Missing, older, newer, mixed | EldenNet keeps playing with its own surfaces in every case. Missing: no menu, a notice on F5, settings in the file. Mixed or unreadable files, or another ABI or protocol major: the menu stays off with a notice naming what to install. An older minor works, and settings whose home page it lacks appear under Settings › All settings as "Newer settings"; a newer minor works with the capabilities EldenNet announces. The browser follows the same rules for the web app (§2.9) (Replaced by D88 under G15 (a): EldenNet's launcher checks and installs before start, and a failed connect leaves EldenNet headless; the rules for older and newer minors stand, §2.9) | Refusing to start; loading whatever is there | EldenNet's subtitles, chat box and call-outs never depend on GraceUI, and a player always has a way to their settings | §2.8, §2.9 |
| D55 | The bridge in process | The same JSON frames as the WebSocket (§6.3) cross `graceui.dll`'s C ABI: the core encodes each revision once and hands it to GraceUI on T6, where it is parsed; commands go out through a bounded ring with a non-blocking send. A binary encoding only as a later capability if §8's budget needs it (Per space since D75 and D81: every frame carries its space, and frames cross with `publish` and `poll` on the space's own thread, §2.9) | Typed C++ values; a binary encoding now | C++ types do not cross separately built DLLs; one encoding, one fuzzer and one set of golden frames serve both views | §6.2, §2.9 |
| D56 | Drawing across the boundary | `graceui.dll` lays out and tessellates; `grace-host` checks each frame's vertex list (counts, indices, clip rectangles, texture ids) and copies it into ImGui's draw list through `hudhook::imgui`; `graceui.dll` links no hudhook, ImGui or MinHook, installs no hook, starts no thread and opens no socket or file. Follows G10 (b), decided by the owner (Superseded in part under G15 (a): `graceui.dll` is the host (D69), and this checker checks every consumer's draw lists, GraceUI's own menu included (D76)) (Later, 2026-11-16: the host copies into Dear ImGui's own draw list, hudhook gone, D107) | One ImGui context shared by two DLLs; GraceUI calling ImGui through a host API | ImGui's context and allocator are per copy of the library; a second hudhook brings a second MinHook (ER02 §7.1); a narrow contract changes rarely | G10 (b) |

| D59 | The off switch | `dashboard.enabled`, on by default, homed in Settings › Browser view; off means no socket (GraceUI's own setting, D84; the player's switch wins over any space's `browser = "always"`, D81) | No switch | Always on is the owner's default; a player who wants no listening port, such as on a Deck in game mode, can still have none | Owner, G4 |
| D60 | Loopback sign-in | A session cookie (256 random bits, new at each game start; `HttpOnly`, `SameSite=Strict`, host-only, no expiry) is set on any top-level page load that arrives on the loopback listener with a loopback `Host` (never on the network listener, D66); API calls and the WebSocket ticket need it as well as `Origin` and Fetch Metadata; the page acts on nothing in its URL. Changes D31 in part (GraceUI's server since G17 (a); the cookie and its rules unchanged) (Later, 2026-11-16, D112: a page load on `localhost` or `[::1]` is redirected to `127.0.0.1` first, so the cookie is set there alone) | No credential on loopback, the other checks only; keeping the token in the link | The player types the address and nothing else (G4); the cookie is a second lock beside Host and Origin against web pages and survives a reload; what limits other programs is §7.1's | §7.2; REN §6 |
